# WPF_ENUM_CATALOG.md — full enum inventory + VAML drift diff (engine contract)

> **gap_wpf_enum_catalog.py (generated).** The complete catalog of every `enum` declared in
> the REAL WPF source (`E:\GitRepos\wpf` — PresentationCore, PresentationFramework, WindowsBase —
> the VAML-facing UI assemblies), with each member's **WPF-true ordinal**. This is the living
> reference a VAML author diffs against to reduce enum-ordinal drift (the exact class of bug
> Sprint 11p / `EnumDomainRegistry` was built to kill — e.g. `Dock` (Left=0) vs
> `HorizontalAlignment` (Left=1)).
>
> Generated by re-running `python gap_wpf_enum_catalog.py` whenever WPF advances; the OUTPUT
> is committed, NOT the stdout. Do NOT hand-edit generated rows; re-run the generator (or add
> under the documented closure rule).
>
> **Scope:** `2882` .cs files scanned; **647 enum declarations** cataloged.

## DRIFT DIFF — VAML EnumDomainRegistry vs WPF ground truth

For each domain VAML registers, this compares VAML's ordinal for every member against the
WPF enum of the same name. **ORDINAL DRIFT** = VAML's ordinal differs from WPF's; **MISSING** = 
a member WPF has that VAML's registration table lacks. A member WPF does NOT have (VAML-invented)
is flagged **EXTRA**.

| VAML key | WPF enum | Verdict |
|----------|----------|---------|
| `DockPanel.Dock` | `Dock` | OK (4 members match WPF) |
| `HorizontalAlignment` | `HorizontalAlignment` | OK (4 members match WPF) |
| `IconStretch` | `IconStretch` | **WPF ENUM NOT FOUND** — no `enum IconStretch` in scan roots |
| `Orientation` | `Orientation` | OK (2 members match WPF) |
| `ResizeMode` | `ResizeMode` | OK (4 members match WPF) |
| `SizeToContent` | `SizeToContent` | OK (4 members match WPF) |
| `StretchDirection` | `StretchDirection` | OK (3 members match WPF) |
| `TextAlignment` | `TextAlignment` | OK (4 members match WPF) |
| `TextTrimming` | `TextTrimming` | OK (3 members match WPF) |
| `TextWrapping` | `TextWrapping` | OK (3 members match WPF) |
| `VerticalAlignment` | `VerticalAlignment` | OK (4 members match WPF) |
| `Visibility` | `Visibility` | OK (3 members match WPF) |
| `WindowStartupLocation` | `WindowStartupLocation` | OK (3 members match WPF) |
| `WindowState` | `WindowState` | **⚠ 2** — MISSING `Theatre` (WPF=3); MISSING `FullScreen` (WPF=4) |
| `WindowStyle` | `WindowStyle` | OK (4 members match WPF) |

### Reading the drift table (caveats)

- **Resolved: `DockPanel.Dock` no longer registers `Fill=4`.** WPF's `Dock` enum is exactly
  `{Left=0, Top=1, Right=2, Bottom=3}` — there is NO `Fill`. VAML previously registered
  `Fill` as both a member and the DP's default; that drift was removed so `Dock` now matches
  WPF member-for-member (the last-child fill is the positional rule + the `LastChildFill`
  bool, never a Dock value).
- **`IconStretch` is a VAML ALIAS, not a WPF enum.** WPF has no `IconStretch` type — the
  members `{None=0, Fill=1, Uniform=2, UniformToFill=3}` exactly mirror WPF's `Stretch`
  enum (`PresentationCore\System\Windows\Media\Generated\Stretch.cs`). Not a defect, but it
  is an invented name: the WPF-faithful type is `Stretch`.
- **Members inside a `#if THEATRE_FULLSCREEN` / `#if DEBUG` region** (e.g. `WindowState.Theatre=3` /
  `FullScreen=4`) are listed as MISSING because they are not part of a normal build. They are
  not shipped drift — a VAML author should only add them if VAML also supports the symbol, and
  NOTE these regions carry no trailing comma, which is why they parse as separate members.

## Full enum catalog

Non-Flags enums. `[Flags]` enums are marked with `🎏` and their members are the bit values.
The ordinal shown is the WPF-true value. * = ordinal computed by the generator (sequential /
explicit-expression best-effort); a non-numeric expression that could not be resolved is
shown as the sequential fallback.
